{"agentId":"a208d6f6ca861d448","cwd":"/home/user/DesignPattern/kindle/design-patterns","entrypoint":"remote_mobile","gitBranch":"main","isSidechain":true,"message":{"content":[{"content":"CODE_CSS = r\"\"\"\n<style>\nhtml, body {\n  margin: 0;\n  padding: 0;\n  background: #0F172A !important;\n  width: __WIDTH__px !important;\n  max-width: __WIDTH__px !important;\n  box-sizing: border-box;\n  overflow: hidden;\n}\n.highlight {\n  background: #0F172A !important;\n  color: #FFFFFF !important;\n  width: __WIDTH__px !important;\n  box-sizing: border-box;\n  padding: 0 40px !important;\n  font-family: \"BIZ UDGothic\", \"MS Gothic\", \"Noto Sans Mono CJK JP\", \"Noto Sans JP\", \"Yu Gothic\", Meiryo, monospace !important;\n  font-size: 48px !important;\n  line-height: 1.6 !important;\n  font-weight: 500 !important;\n}\n.highlight pre {\n  margin: 0 !important;\n  padding: 10px 0 !important;\n  background: #0F172A !important;\n  color: #FFFFFF !important;\n  white-space: pre-wrap !important;\n  overflow-wrap: break-word !important;\n  word-wrap: break-word !important;\n  word-break: break-all !important;\n  font: inherit !important;\n}\n.highlight .c1, .highlight .cm, .highlight .c {\n  color: #64748B !important;\n  font-weight: 600 !important;\n  font-style: italic !important;\n}\n.highlight .cp {\n  color: #C4B5FD !important;\n--- split_chunks\ndef split_chunks(\n    lines: Sequence[str],\n    has_title: bool,\n    first_limit: int,\n    continued_limit: int,\n    orphan_threshold: int,\n) -> list[list[str]]:\n    \"\"\"Split display lines while avoiding a tiny final image.\"\"\"\n    if not lines:\n        return [[\"\"]]\n    chunks: list[list[str]] = []\n    start = 0\n    while start < len(lines):\n        limit = first_limit if not chunks and has_title else continued_limit\n        remaining = len(lines) - start\n        size = min(remaining, limit)\n        tail = remaining - size\n        if 0 < tail <= orphan_threshold:\n            size = remaining\n        chunks.append(list(lines[start : start + size]))\n        start += size\n    return chunks\n\n\ndef strip_markdown(text: str) -> str:\n    text = re.sub(r\"!\\[([^\\]]*)\\]\\([^)]+\\)\", r\"\\1\", text)\n    text = re.sub(r\"\\[([^\\]]+)\\]\\([^)]+\\)\", r\"\\1\", text)\n    text = re.sub(r\"[`*_~]\", \"\", text)\n    return re.sub(r\"\\s+\", \" \", text).strip()\n\n\ndef chapter_title(markdown_text: str, fallback: str) -> str:","is_error":false,"tool_use_id":"toolu_01W41QrGxiG55ux9A3yYg2dW","type":"tool_result"}],"role":"user"},"parentUuid":"6d923b48-2a05-40d1-96c3-930c0e3eae82","promptId":"86d5196f-fe86-46dd-98e4-a44ae88e7283","sessionId":"fff34703-856c-52bd-b27b-6e2609349c4c","slug":"vectorized-napping-dahl","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"6d923b48-2a05-40d1-96c3-930c0e3eae82","timestamp":"2026-09-01T11:30:49.212Z","type":"user","userType":"external","uuid":"36cfb105-0c7e-4ded-b290-4799c0037f22","version":"2.1.252"}

{"agentId":"a208d6f6ca861d448","attributionAgent":"general-purpose","cwd":"/home/user/DesignPattern/kindle/design-patterns","effort":"high","entrypoint":"remote_mobile","gitBranch":"main","isSidechain":true,"message":{"content":[{"caller":{"type":"direct"},"id":"toolu_014HDzpfA8geKrJGXHnpkcVz","input":{"command":"python3 - <<'EOF'\nimport re,os,unicodedata\nBASE=\"output\"\nCH=[\"chapter00_1.md\",\"chapter00_2.md\"]+[\"chapter%02d.md\"%i for i in range(1,9)]\ndef dw(s): return sum(2 if unicodedata.east_asian_width(c) in \"WF\" else 1 for c in s)\nrows=[]\nfor f in CH:\n    lines=open(os.path.join(BASE,f),encoding=\"utf-8\").read().split(\"\\n\")\n    inf=False;lang=\"\"\n    for i,l in enumerate(lines):\n        m=re.match(r'^\\s*```(\\w*)',l)\n        if m:\n            inf=not inf\n            if inf: lang=m.group(1)\n            continue\n        if inf and lang==\"cpp\":\n            s=l.rstrip()\n            if len(s)<=80 and dw(s)>80: rows.append((dw(s),len(s),f,i+1,s))\nrows.sort(reverse=True)\nprint(\"ゲート(80文字)は通るが描画幅80桁を超えて折り返す行:\",len(rows))\nfor w,n,f,ln,s in rows[:12]:\n    print(f\"  {w}桁/{n}文字  {f}:L{ln}  {s.strip()[:70]}\")\nEOF","description":"List wrapping code lines"},"name":"Bash","type":"tool_use"}],"diagnostics":null,"id":"msg_011CeceJ6VU4pgB6P6wMdXkr","model":"claude-opus-5","role":"assistant","stop_details":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"type":"message","usage":{"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1726},"cache_creation_input_tokens":1726,"cache_read_input_tokens":139055,"inference_geo":"not_available","input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard"}},"parentUuid":"1f95c1b9-64c5-421b-b9cf-fd93ad68cee3","requestId":"req_011CeceJ4vD2V5RkEe45EwH2","sessionId":"fff34703-856c-52bd-b27b-6e2609349c4c","slug":"vectorized-napping-dahl","timestamp":"2026-09-01T11:31:00.695Z","type":"assistant","userType":"external","uuid":"a92f363b-5ec8-4359-8b69-2d7e6b6b5ef9","version":"2.1.252"}
